function TKundenImport.GetMvavPk(ATable: TTableObj): String;
var column, mvaId, vbaNr, mvavBeginn: TColumnObj;
    i: Integer;
begin
  mvaId := nil;
  vbaNr := nil;
  mvavBeginn := nil;
  for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
  begin
    column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
    if column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID' then
      mvaId := column
    else
    if column.Columnname = 'VBA_NR' then
      vbaNr := column
    else
    if column.Columnname = 'MVAV_BEGINN' then
      mvavBeginn := column;
  end;
  if (mvaId = nil) or (mvavBeginn = nil) or (vbaNr = nil) then
    raise Exception.Create('Interner Fehler, PK in ENMVAV-Daten nicht vollständig');
  result := mvaId.Value + ';' + vbaNr.Value + ';' + mvavBeginn.Value;
end;

function TKundenImport.ExecuteQuerySQL(AQuery: TSQLQuery; ATableName: String; AObj: TExpImpTableObj): Boolean;
var
  sqlList: TelStringStringMap;
  i: Integer;

  procedure ExecSqlIntern(AQuery: TSQLQuery; Ai: Integer);
  begin
    AQuery.SQL.Clear;
    AQuery.SQL.Add(sqlList.Objects[Ai]);

    try
      AQuery.ExecSQL;
    except
      on e: Exception do
      begin
        if (not FImportOptionen.FehlerIgnorieren) then
        begin
          AddLog(e.Message + CR_LF + CR_LF +
             'Identifier: ' + sqlList.Items[Ai] + CR_LF +
             'Letztes Statement: ' + CR_LF + sqlList.Objects[Ai]);

          raise Exception.Create(e.Message + CR_LF + CR_LF +
             'Identifier: ' + sqlList.Items[Ai] + CR_LF +
             'Letztes Statement: ' + CR_LF + sqlList.Objects[Ai]);
        end
         else
        AddLog('Ignoriert: ' + e.Message + ' / Identifier: ' + sqlList.Items[Ai]);
      end;
    end;
  end;

begin
  SetStatus('Speichere Daten ' + ATableName);
  Result := True;
  sqlList := CreateInsertSelect(ATableName, AObj);
  try
    for i := 0 to sqlList.Count - 1 do
      ExecSqlIntern(AQuery, i);
  finally
    sqlList.Free;
  end;
end;

function TKundenImport.IsRechnungstabelle(tableName: String): Boolean;
begin
  Result := False;
  if ((tableName = ENPRZ)

     or (tableName = EDDAT)
     or (tableName = EDMSC)
     or (tableName = CREPZ)
     or (tableName = ENLSCH)
     or (tableName = ENLSMW)
     or (tableName = ENLSMH)
     or (tableName = EDEPR)

     or (tableName = ENSPZ)
     or (tableName = CRGPVR)

     or (tableName = ENRPR)
     or (tableName = ENKNV)
     or (tableName = ENVFA)
     or (tableName = ENSPZ)
     or (tableName = ENVKZ)
     or (tableName = ENVFM)
     or (tableName = ENVFME)
     or (tableName = ENFDEF)
     or (tableName = ENFSU)
     or (tableName = ENFVB)
     or (tableName = ENMLR)
     or (tableName = ENVFI)
     or (tableName = ENVFS)
     or (tableName = ENGBT)
     or (tableName = ENVFAI)
     or (tableName = ENEAVD)
     or (tableName = ENABSR)
     or (tableName = ENABSZ)
     or (tableName = ENVZR)
     or (tableName = ENVZZ)
     or (tableName = ENESA)
     or (tableName = ENEGA)
     or (tableName = ENEWA)
     or (tableName = ENESAM)
     or (tableName = ENEGAM)
     or (tableName = ENEWAM)
     or (tableName = ENPRDU)
     or (tableName = ENPRDM)
     or (tableName = ENPRDT)
     ) then
  Result := True;
end;

procedure TKundenImport.MerkePrzIdVorabzahlung;
var
  i, j, przId: Integer;
  eanlId, tmpValue: TSequenceInt;
  column: TColumnObj;
  table: TTableObj;
begin
  przId := NULL_INTEGER;
  eanlId := NULL_INTEGER;
  for i := 0 to fTables.Childs.Count - 1 do
  begin
    table := fTables.Childs[i];
    if (table.Tablename = ENEANL) then
    begin
      for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := table.Columns.Items[j];
        if ((column.Columnname = 'EANL_ID') and (column.Value <> '')) then
        begin
          eanlId := StrToInt64(column.Value);
        end
        else if ((column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID_VORABZAHLUNG') and (column.Value <> '')) then
        begin
          przId := StrToInt64(column.Value);
        end;
      end;


      if ((eanlId <> NULL_INTEGER) and (przId <> NULL_INTEGER)) then
      begin
        if (not eanlPrzIdVorabzahlungMapping.TryGetValue(eanlId, tmpValue)) then
        begin
          eanlPrzIdVorabzahlungMapping.Add(eanlId, przId);
        end;
      end;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenImport.MerkeSchlussrechnungPrzId;
var
  i, j, przId: Integer;
  mvaId, tmpValue: TSequenceInt;
  column: TColumnObj;
  childTable: TTableObj;
begin
  for i := 0 to fTables.Childs.Count - 1 do
  begin
    przId := NULL_INTEGER;
    mvaId := NULL_INTEGER;

    childTable := fTables.Childs.Items[i];
    if (childTable.Tablename = ENMVA) then
    begin
      for j := 0 to childTable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := childTable.Columns.Items[j];
        if ((column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID') and (column.Value <> '')) then
        begin
          mvaId := StrToInt64(column.Value);
        end
        else if ((column.Columnname = 'MVA_SCHLUSSREPRZ_ID') and (column.Value <> '')) then
        begin
          przId := StrToInt64(column.Value);
        end;
      end;


      if ((mvaId <> NULL_INTEGER) and (przId <> NULL_INTEGER)) then
      begin
        if (not mvaSchlussrechnungPrzIdMapping.TryGetValue(mvaId, tmpValue)) then
        begin
          mvaSchlussrechnungPrzIdMapping.Add(mvaId, przId);
        end;
      end;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenImport.RegisterTableIdMapping(ATableName, AIdFieldName, ASequenceName: String);
var mapInfoVgl: TTableIdMapping;
begin
  if TableIdMapping.ContainsKey(ATableName) then
    exit;

  for mapInfoVgl in TableIdMapping.ValueCollection do
    if (mapInfoVgl.IdFieldName = AIdFieldName) then
      raise Exception.Create('Interner Fehler, ' + AIdFieldName + '  mehrfach vorhanden!!');

  TableIdMapping.AddObject(ATableName, TTableIdMapping.Create(ATableName, AIdFieldName, ASequenceName));
end;

function TKundenImport.CheckObDatensatzVorhanden(table: TTableObj): Boolean;
var
  i: Integer;
  Id: TSequenceInt;
  column: TColumnObj;
begin
  Result := False;
  for i := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
  begin
    column := TColumnObj(table.Columns.Items[i]);
    if((table.Tablename = ENZPB) and (column.Columnname = 'MES_ID')) then
    begin
      Result := MesIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), Id);
      exit;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenImport.LegeKundenAn(tableName: String);
var
  i, j, k: Integer;
  kndId, kndIdNeu: Int64;
  childTable: TTableObj;
  column, eanlColumn: TColumnObj;
  isAnlagenKnd, anlageInListe: Boolean;
  eanlNrAlt, eanlIdAlt, kndNrAlt, kndNrNeu: String;
  kndEanlZuordnung: TKundenAnlagenZuordnung;
begin
  SetStatus('Lege Kunden an');
  isAnlagenKnd := tableName = ENEANB;
  for i := 0 to fTables.Childs.Count - 1 do
  begin
    childTable := fTables.Childs.Items[i];
    if (childTable.Tablename = tableName) then
    begin
      for j := 0 to childTable.columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := TColumnObj(childTable.Columns.Items[j]);
        if(column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
        begin
          kndId := StrToInt64(column.Value);
          childTable.ImportErledigt := true;
          if (not KndIdMapping.ContainsKey(kndId)) then
          begin
            LegeKundeUndGpAn(childTable, kndId, isAnlagenKnd);
          end
          else
          begin
            if (isAnlagenKnd) then
            begin
              for k := 0 to childTable.Columns.Count - 1 do
              begin
                eanlColumn := childTable.Columns.Items[k];
                if (eanlColumn.Columnname = 'EANL_NR') then
                  eanlNrAlt := EntferneHochkomma(eanlColumn.Value)
                else if (eanlColumn.Columnname = 'EANL_ID') then
                  eanlIdAlt := eanlColumn.Value;
              end;
            end;
            anlageInListe := False;
            for k := 0 to kndEanlList.Count - 1 do
            begin
              kndEanlZuordnung := kndEanlList.Items[k] as TKundenAnlagenZuordnung;
              if (kndEanlZuordnung.fKndIdAlt = kndId) then
              begin
                anlageInListe := True;
                break;
              end;
            end;
            if (not anlageInListe) then
            begin
              kndEanlZuordnung := TKundenAnlagenZuordnung.Create;
              kndEanlZuordnung.fMndId := fMndId;
              if (not StringEmpty(eanlIdAlt)) then
              begin
                kndEanlZuordnung.feanlIdAlt := StrToInt64(eanlIdAlt);
                kndEanlZuordnung.feanlNrAlt := eanlNrAlt;
              end;
              if (KndIdMapping.ContainsKey(kndId)) then
              begin
                kndEanlZuordnung.fKndIdAlt  := kndId;
                KndIdMapping.TryGetValue(kndId, kndIdNeu);
                kndEanlZuordnung.fKndIdNeu := kndIdNeu;
              end;
              if (KndIdAltKndNrAltMapping.ContainsKey(kndId)) then
              begin
                KndIdAltKndNrAltMapping.TryGetValue(kndId, kndNrAlt);
                kndEanlZuordnung.fKndNrAlt := kndNrAlt;
              end;
              if (KndIdAltKndNrNeuMapping.ContainsKey(kndId)) then
              begin
                KndIdAltKndNrNeuMapping.TryGetValue(kndId, kndNrNeu);
                kndEanlZuordnung.fKndNrNeu := kndNrNeu;
              end;
              if (isAnlagenKnd) then
              begin
                kndEanlZuordnung.fIsKreditor := True;
              end;
              kndEanlList.Add(kndEanlZuordnung);

              if (isAnlagenKnd) then
              begin
                if (not fEinspeiseKunden.Contains(kndIdNeu)) then
                  fEinspeiseKunden.Add(kndIdNeu);
              end
              else
              begin
                if (not fBezugsKunden.Contains(kndIdNeu)) then
                  fBezugsKunden.Add(kndIdNeu);
              end;
            end;
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end;
  end;
end;

function TKundenImport.CheckSpaltenOk(ATable: TTableObj): boolean;
var
  i: Integer;
  column: TColumnObj;
  dTable: TDBModelTable;
begin
  Result := True;
  if (not fKompatibilitaetsmodus) then
    exit;


  dTable := TDBModelUtils.GetTables.Find(ATable.Tablename);
  if dTable = nil then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('Tabelle ' + ATable.Tablename + ' nicht in DB-Modell gefunden');
  for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
  begin
    column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
    if (todoFelder.IndexOf(column.Columnname) > -1) then
      continue;

    if dTable.Columns.Find(column.Columnname) = nil then
    begin
      result := false;
      exit;
    end;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenImport.AddLog(AMessage: String);
begin
  if (flog = nil) or (flog.IndexOf(AMessage) > 0) then
    exit;
  flog.Add(AMessage);
end;

function TKundenImport.CheckColumnExists(ATablename, AColumnName: String): boolean;
var
  dTable: TDBModelTable;

begin
  Result := True;

  if ((AColumnName = 'GER_GERAETEGRUNDTYP_MASTER')
      or (AColumnName = 'GER_GERAETEGRUNDTYP_SLAVE')) then
  begin
    Result := False;
    exit;
  end;

  if (not fKompatibilitaetsmodus) then
    exit;

  dTable := TDBModelUtils.GetTables.Find(ATablename);
  if dTable = nil then
    raise EInvalidOperation.Create('Tabelle ' + ATablename + ' nicht in DB-Modell gefunden');

  if dTable.Columns.Find(AColumnname) = nil then
    result := false;
end;

function TKundenImport.CreateInsertSelect(ATablename: String; AObj: TExpImpTableObj): TelStringStringMap;
var
  table: TTableObj;
  column: TColumnObj;
  sql, sql2, value: String;
  aaufId: Int64;
  i, j: Integer;
  tableList: TList;
  spaltenAlleVorhanden, ignorieren: Boolean;

  PrzIdTableMap: TelLongIdObjectMap<TTableObj>;
  PrzId: TLongId;


  function GetIdentifier: String;
  var
    column: TColumnObj;
    j: Integer;
  begin
    result := '';
    for j := 0 to min(table.Columns.Count - 1, 3) do
    begin
      column := table.Columns.Items[j];
      result := IfThen(J>0, result + ' / ', '') + column.Columnname + ' = "' + column.Value + '"';
    end;
  end;

begin
  Result := TelStringStringMap.Create;

  sql := NULL_STRING;
  sql2 := NULL_STRING;
  tableList := TList.Create;
  try
    if (AObj <> nil) and (AObj.ImportValues.count > 0) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to AObj.ImportValues.Count - 1 do
        if AObj.CanDoImport(AObj.ImportValues[i], self) then
          tableList.Add(AObj.ImportValues[i]);
    end
     else
    for i := 0 to fTables.Childs.Count - 1 do
    begin
      table := fTables.Childs.Items[i];

      if (table.ImportErledigt) then
        continue;

      if (table.Tablename = ATablename) then
        tableList.Add(table);
    end;

    if (ENPRZ = ATablename) then
    begin
      // Falls OwnsObjects auf True gesetzt werden, sind die Pointer zu Tables verloren
      PrzIdTableMap := TelLongIdObjectMap<TTableObj>.Create;
      try
        if (tableList.Count > 0) then
        begin
          for i:= 0 to tableList.Count - 1 do
          begin
            table := tableList.Items[i];
            for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
            begin
              column := table.Columns.Items[j];
              if (Column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
              begin
                PrzId := TLongId.FromStr(column.value);
                PrzIdTableMap.Add(PrzId, table);
                Break;
              end;
            end;
          end;

          tableList.Clear;

          for i := 0 to PrzIdTableMap.Count - 1 do
          begin
            PrzId := PrzIdTableMap.Items[I];
            table := PrzIdTableMap.Values[PrzId];
            tableList.Add(table);
          end;
        end;
      finally
        PrzIdTableMap.Free;
      end;
    end;

    for i := 0 to tableList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      ignorieren := False;
      table := tableList.Items[i];
      SetStatus('Speichere Daten ' + ATablename + ', verarbeite '+ intToStr(i) + ' / ' + IntToStr(tableList.Count));
      if TableIdMapping.ContainsKey(table.Tablename) then
      begin
         if (SchonAufDBVorhanden(table, AObj)) then
         begin
           table.ImportErledigt := true;
           ignorieren := True;
         end;
      end
       else
      if (table.Tablename = ENMVA) then
      begin
        if (not CheckVbaNrVorhanden(table)) then
        begin
          for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
          begin
            column := table.Columns.Items[j];
            if (column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID') then
            begin
              zuIgnorierendeMvaIds.Add(StrToInt64(column.Value));
              AddLog('MvaId ' + column.Value + ' nicht importiert, da VbaNr nicht vorhanden');
              break;
            end;
          end;
          continue;
        end;
      end
       else
      if (table.Tablename = ENMVAV) then
      begin
        if (not CheckVbaNrVorhanden(table)) then
        begin
          AddLog('Mvav ' + GetMvavPk(table) + ' nicht importiert, da VbaNr nicht vorhanden');
          continue;
        end;
        for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
        begin
          column := table.Columns.Items[j];
          if (column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID') then
          begin
            if zuIgnorierendeMvaIds.Contains(StrToInt64(column.Value)) then
            begin
              AddLog('Mvav ' + GetMvavPk(table) + ' nicht importiert, da MvaId ignoriert');
              continue;
            end;
            break;
          end;
        end;
      end
      else if ((table.Tablename = ENZPG)
               or (table.Tablename = ENZPZ)
               or (table.Tablename = ENZPTH)
               or (table.Tablename = ENVFM)
               or (table.Tablename = ENFSU)
               or (table.Tablename = ENFVB)
               or (table.Tablename = ENMLR)
               or (table.Tablename = ENVFI)
               or (table.Tablename = ENVFS)
               or (table.Tablename = ENGBT)
               or (table.Tablename = ENZAE)
               or (table.Tablename = ENZZK)
               or (table.Tablename = ENVBA)
               or (table.Tablename = ENABNGMD)
               or (table.Tablename = ENVET)
               or (table.Tablename = ENFLD)
               or (table.Tablename = ENZPTTREANL)
               or (table.Tablename = ENGERP)
               or (table.Tablename = ENEAVSV)
               ) then
      begin
         if (SchonAufDBVorhanden(table, AObj)) then
         begin
           table.ImportErledigt := true;
           ignorieren := True;
         end;
      end
       else
      if (table.Tablename = ENAMMR) then
      begin
        // Evtl. wird nur die aauf_id_melo oder nur die aauf_id_malo exportiert
        // Falls eine von beiden Fehlt, dann hier den Import ignornieren
       for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
        begin
          column := table.Columns.Items[j];
          if (column.Columnname = 'AAUF_ID_MELO') or
             (column.Columnname = 'AAUF_ID_MALO') then
          begin
            if not enaaufIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64Def(column.Value, NULL_INT64), aaufId) then
            begin
              AddLog('Datensatz aus ENAMMR (' + GetIdentifier + ') nicht importiert, ' + column.Columnname + ' = ' + column.Value + ' wurde nicht exportiert');
              ignorieren := True;
              break;
            end;
          end;
        end;
      end;

      if ((not ignorieren)
          and FImportOptionen.RechnungenImportieren) then
      begin

        // Falls Prozess ignoriert wird, wird die Logmeldung ausgegeben.
        // Durch das Ändern von VON und BIS Datum können auch die Ignorierte Prozesse importiert werden
        ignorieren := CheckRechnungsterminIgnorieren(table);
      end;

      if (ignorieren) then
        continue;

      if (SchonImportiert(table)) then
        continue;

      if (table.Tablename = ENZPB) then
      begin
        if (not CheckObDatensatzVorhanden(table)) then
          continue;
      end
      else if (table.Tablename = ENZPO) then
      begin
        ErsetzeVbsId(table);
      end
      else if (table.Tablename = ENMVAV) then
        mvavPkImportiert.Add(GetMvavPk(table));

      sql := NULL_STRING;
      sql2 := NULL_STRING;
      if ((table.Tablename = ENNBT)
        or (table.Tablename = ENRGZ)
        or (table.Tablename = ENMES)) then
      begin
        LegeKundenAn(table.Tablename);
      end;

      table.ImportErledigt := true;
      spaltenAlleVorhanden := CheckSpaltenOk(table);
      for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := table.Columns.Items[j];

        if (todoFelder.IndexOf(column.Columnname) > -1) then
        begin
          if column.value <> '' then
            AddLog(table.Tablename + ', Ignorierte Spalte ' +  column.Columnname + ': Wert ' + column.value + ' wird nicht importiert!');
          continue;
        end;

        if ((not spaltenAlleVorhanden) and (not CheckColumnExists(table.Tablename, column.Columnname))) then
          continue;

        if AObj = nil then
          FillIdMapping(ATablename, table, column)
        else
          AObj.PreparePksForImport(fConnection, table, self);
        value := GetValue(column, table, AObj);

        if ((ATablename = ENZPT) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ENERGIEART')) then
        begin
          ergaenzeZptEnergieartMap(table);
        end else
        if (table.Tablename = CREPZ) and (column.Columnname = 'EPZ_BEZEICHNUNG') then
        begin
          value := '!KndImportPlatzhalter! ' + value;
          if length(value) > 255 then
            value := Copy('!KndImportPlatzhalter! ' + value, 1, 250) + '...';
          value := QuotedStr(value);
        end else
        if (table.Tablename = CREPZ) and (column.Columnname = 'EPZ_NOTE') then
        begin
          value := QuotedStr('!KndImportPlatzhalter! Nur CREPZ importiert, sonst nichts!')
        end;

        if (((column.Columnname = 'ZPG_ID')
            or (column.Columnname = 'ZPG_ID_MASTER')
            or (column.Columnname = 'ZPG_ID_SLAVE')
            or (column.Columnname = 'ZPZ_ID')
            )
           and ((table.Tablename = ENZPGD)
                or (table.Tablename = ENGBZ)
                or (table.Tablename = ENZPZ)
                or (table.Tablename = ENZPS))) then
        begin
          if ((value = '') or (Trim(value) = 'NULL') or (StrToInt(value) = NULL_INTEGER)) then
          begin
            sql := '';
            break;
          end;
        end;

        if ((column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID')
            and (IsRechnungstabelle(table.Tablename) or (table.Tablename = ENEPEXRI))) then
        begin
          if ((value = '') or (Trim(value) = 'NULL') or (StrToInt(value) = NULL_INTEGER)) then
          begin
            sql := '';
            break;
          end;
        end;

        if ((column.Columnname = 'EANL_ID')
            and (table.Tablename = ENZPTTREANL)) then
        begin
          if ((value = '') or (Trim(value) = 'NULL') or (StrToInt(value) = NULL_INTEGER)) then
          begin
            sql := '';
            break;
          end;
        end;

        // Vertragsparameter von nicht exportierten Kunden ignorieren
        if ((column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') and (table.Tablename = ENKVP)) then
        begin
          if ((value = '') or (Trim(value) = 'NULL') or (StrToInt(value) = NULL_INTEGER)) then
          begin
            sql := '';
            break;
          end;
        end;

        if (sql = NULL_STRING) then
        begin
          sql := column.Columnname;
        end
        else
        begin
          sql := sql + ', ' + column.Columnname;
        end;

        if ((table.Tablename = ENMLK)
            and (column.Columnname = 'MLK_CODE')
            and (fImportiereMlk.IndexOf(value) = -1)) then
        begin
          fImportiereMlk.Add(value);
        end;

        if ((table.Tablename = ENTRE)
            and (column.Columnname = 'TRE_TR_ID')
            and (fImportiereTre.IndexOf(value) = -1)) then
        begin
          fImportiereTre.Add(value);
        end;

        if (column.columnname = 'ERVZT_TR_ID') then
          value := QuotedStr(value);

        if (value = '') then
          value := ' NULL ';
        if (sql2 = NULL_STRING) then
        begin
          sql2 := 'SELECT ' + value + ' AS ' + column.Columnname;
        end
        else
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ', ' + value + ' AS ' + column.Columnname;
        end;
        if (table.Tablename = ENEANL) then
        begin
          if (column.Columnname = 'EANL_NR') then
            eanlNrNeu := EntferneHochkomma(value)
          else if (column.Columnname = 'EANL_ABRBEGINN') then
            fAbrBeginn := getDateFromValue(value);
        end;
      end;
      if (sql <> '') then
      begin
        sql2 := sql2 + ' FROM dual ';
        sql := 'INSERT INTO ' + ATablename + ' ( ' + sql + ') ';
        sql := sql + sql2;
      end;
      if((ATablename = ENNNB)) then
      begin
        if (SchonAufDBVorhanden(table, AObj)) then
         continue;
      end;
      //ENTFERNEN IN NÄCHSTER VERSION
      if ((table.Tablename = ENZMB)
          and (pos('ZMB_ID', sql) = 0)) then
      begin
        //weg lassen
        continue;
      end;
      if (sql <> '') then
      begin
        Result.AddObject(GetIdentifier, sql);
      end;
    end;
  finally
    tableList.Free;
  end;
end;

function TKundenImport.CheckRechnungsterminIgnorieren(table: TTableObj): Boolean;
var
  i: Integer;
  column: TColumnObj;
  valueAsDate: TDateTime;
  przArt: Integer;
  przId, meldung: String;

begin
  Result := False;
  if (table.Tablename = ENPRZ) then
  begin
    valueAsDate := NULL_DATE;
    przArt := NULL_INTEGER;
    for i := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
    begin
      column := table.Columns.Items[i];
      if (column.Value <> '') then
      begin
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_TERMIN') then
        begin
          valueAsDate := getDateFromValue(column.Value);
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ART') then
        begin
          przArt := StrToInt64(column.Value);
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
        begin
          przId := column.Value;
        end;
      end;
    end;
    if ((przArt = 51) and (valueAsDate = NULL_DATE)) then
    begin
      valueAsDate := FImportOptionen.StichtagBis;
    end;
    if (valueAsDate <> NULL_DATE) then
    begin
      if (FImportOptionen.StichtagVon > valueAsDate) then
      begin
        Result := True;
        meldung := '"Stichdatum "Von" für Rechnungsprozesse" > PRZ_TERMIN';
      end
      else if (FImportOptionen.StichtagBis < valueAsDate) then
      begin
        Result := True;
        meldung := '"Stichdatum "Bis" für Rechnungsprozesse" < PRZ_TERMIN';
      end
      else if ((FImportOptionen.StichtagBis = valueAsDate)
             and FImportOptionen.LetzteRechnungNurTeilRechnung
             and ((przArt = 2) or (przArt = 3))) then
      begin
        Result := True;
        meldung := ' ("Stichdatum "Bis" für Rechnungsprozesse" = PRZ_TERMIN) und (przArt ist GR oder SR) und zum Stichtag "Bis" werden nicht nur Teilrechnungen beachtet';
      end;
    end;
  end;
  if (Result) then
  begin
    AddLog(CR_LF);
    AddLog('=================================== PROZESS IGNORIERT ==================================');
    AddLog('ENProzess mit PRZ_ID ' + przId + ' wurde ignoriert und wird nicht importiert weil ' + meldung);
    AddLog(CR_LF);
  end;
end;

function TKundenImport.SchonAufDBVorhanden(ATable: TTableObj; AObj: TExpImpTableObj): Boolean;
var
  i: Integer;
  column: TColumnObj;
  sql1, sql2: String;
  qry: TSQLQuery;
  id: Int64;
  mapInfo: TTableIdMapping;

begin
  Result := False;
  qry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    sql1 := 'SELECT * FROM ' + ATable.Tablename + ' WHERE 1 = 1 ';
    sql2 := '';

    mapInfo := TableIdMapping.Values[ATable.Tablename];
    if mapInfo <> nil then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = mapInfo.IdFieldName) then
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ' + mapInfo.IdFieldName + ' ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj, false));
          break;
        end;
      end;
      if (sql2 = '') then
        raise Exception.Create('SchonAufDBVorhanden(' + ATable.Tablename + '): Spalte ' + mapInfo.IdFieldName + ' nicht in aktueller Tabelle gefunden!');
    end
    else
    if (ATable.Tablename = ENNNB) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'NET_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND net_id ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'NBT_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND nbt_id ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'NNB_GUELTIGAB') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND nnb_gueltigab ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
      end;
    end
    else
    if (ATable.Tablename = ENNWS) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'NBT_ID_NEU') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND nbt_id ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENZPZ) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'ZPG_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND zpg_id ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPZ_OBISCODE') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND zpz_obiscode ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENZPTH) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'ZPTH_ABRECHNUNGSZPT_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZPTH_ABRECHNUNGSZPT_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZPT_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPTH_GUELTIGAB') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZPTH_GUELTIGAB ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENVFM) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND KND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENFSU) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND KND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'FSU_LFDNR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND FSU_LFDNR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENFVB) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND KND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'FVB_LFDNR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND FVB_LFDNR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENMLR) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'MES_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MES_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENVFI) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND KND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'VFI_LFDNR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VFI_LFDNR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENVFS) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND KND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'VFS_LFDNR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VFS_LFDNR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENGBT) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND PRZ_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND KND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'GBT_LFDNR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND GBT_LFDNR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENZAE) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'ZAE_NR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZAE_NR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj))
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENZZK) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'ZZK_ZAEHLWERKNR') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZZK_ZAEHLWERKNR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ZZK_BEGINN') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZZK_BEGINN ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ZZK_ENDE') then
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZZK_ENDE ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj))
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ZAE_ID') then
        begin
          if (enzaeIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ZAE_ID = ' + IntToStr(id);
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENVBA) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'VBA_NR') then
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VBA_NR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'MND_ID') then
        begin
          if (FImportOptionen.StammdatenWieExport) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(fMndId) + ' ';
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENABNGMD) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'ABN_ID') then
        begin
          if (abnIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ABN_ID = ' + IntToStr(id);
          end;
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ABNGMD_GUELTIGAB') then
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ABNGMD_GUELTIGAB ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENVER) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj) <> '') then
        begin
          if (column.Columnname = 'VET_NR') then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VET_NR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else if (column.Columnname = 'VEA_NR') then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VEA_NR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else if (column.Columnname = 'VER_NR') then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VER_NR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else if (column.Columnname = 'VER_BEZEICHNUNG') then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VER_BEZEICHNUNG ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else if (column.Columnname = 'MND_ID') then
          begin
            if (FImportOptionen.StammdatenWieExport) then
            begin
              sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
            end
            else
            begin
              sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(fMndId) + ' ';
            end;
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENVET) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'VET_NR') then
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND VET_NR ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'MND_ID') then
        begin
          if (FImportOptionen.StammdatenWieExport) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(fMndId) + ' ';
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENFLD) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'FLD_NAME') then
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND FLD_NAME ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'MND_ID') then
        begin
          if (FImportOptionen.StammdatenWieExport) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
          end
          else
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND MND_ID = ' + IntToStr(fMndId) + ' ';
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENZPTTREANL) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'EANL_ID') then
        begin
          if (eanlIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND EANL_ID = ' + IntToStr(id);
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENGERP) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'GERP_ID') then
        begin
          if (gerpIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND GERP_ID = ' + IntToStr(id);
          end;
        end
        else if (column.Columnname = 'ABN_ID') then
        begin
          sql2 := sql2 + ' AND ABN_ID ' + GetValueForSelect(GetValue(Column, ATable, AObj));
        end;
      end;
    end
    else if (ATable.Tablename = ENEAVSV) then
    begin
      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (column.Columnname = 'EAVSV_ID') then
        begin
          if (eavsIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)) then
          begin
            sql2 := sql2 + ' AND EAVSV_ID = ' + IntToStr(id);
          end;
        end;
      end;
    end;
    if (sql2 <> '') then
    begin
      qry.SQL.Add(sql1 + sql2);
      qry.Open;
      if (not qry.Eof) then
        Result := True;
      qry.Close;
    end;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;


procedure TKundenImport.FillIdMapping(tableName: String; table: TTableObj; column: TColumnObj);
var
  neueId: Int64;
  i: Integer;
  kndEanlZuordnung: TKundenAnlagenZuordnung;
  mapInfo: TTableIdMapping;
begin

  if ((column.Columnname = 'ZPO_KONFIG_ID') or (column.Columnname = 'SMA_KONFIGID')) then
  begin
    // Da hier Strings gespeichert werden, werden IDs nicht gemappt,
    // sonst kommt den Fehler bei StrToInt64. Mapping wird direkt in FZpoIdKonfig befüllt.
    Exit;
  end;

  if TableIdMapping.ContainsKey(tableName) then
  begin
    mapInfo := TableIdMapping.Values[tableName];
    if (mapInfo.IdFieldName = column.Columnname) then
    begin
      // Generiert ggf. einen neuen PK
      FillIdMap(mapInfo.IdMapping, StrToInt64(EntferneHochkomma(column.value)), mapInfo.SequenceName);
      exit;
    end;
  end;

  for mapInfo in TableIdMapping.ValueCollection do
    if mapInfo.IsRelevant(tableName, column.Columnname) then
    begin
      if (mapInfo.IdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(EntferneHochkomma(column.value)), neueId)) then
        exit;
    end;

  if ((tableName = ENABN) and (column.Columnname = 'ABN_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(AbnIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENABN);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPT) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZptIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENMES) and (column.Columnname = 'MES_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(MesIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENKND) and (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(KndIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENKUNDE);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEANL) and (column.Columnname = 'EANL_ID')) then
  begin
    neueId := FillIdMap(EanlIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
    for i := 0 to kndEanlList.Count - 1 do
    begin
      kndEanlZuordnung := kndEanlList.Items[i] as TKundenAnlagenZuordnung;
      if (kndEanlZuordnung.feanlIdAlt = StrToInt64(column.Value)) then
      begin
        kndEanlZuordnung.feanlIdNeu := neueId;
        break;
      end;
    end;
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEANB) and (column.Columnname = 'EANB_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EanbIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAV) and (column.Columnname = 'EAV_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EavIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAVS) and (column.Columnname = 'EAVS_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EavsIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENMVA) and (column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(MvaIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENMVA);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPO) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPO_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpoIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZPTOBIS);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPOE) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPOE_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpoeIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZPTOBIS);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPZ) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPZ_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpzIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLPUNKTZAEHLWERKE);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPS) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPS_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpsIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLPUNKTZAEHLWERKE);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEZPA) and (column.Columnname = 'EZPA_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EzpaIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAD) and (column.Columnname = 'EAD_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EadIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAPW) and (column.Columnname = 'EAPW_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EapwIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPB) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPB_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpbIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZPTDATEN);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPBD) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPBD_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpbdIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZPTDATEN);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPN) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPN_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpnIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZPTDATEN);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAB) and (column.Columnname = 'EAB_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EabIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEABT) and (column.Columnname = 'EABT_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EabtIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEABP) and (column.Columnname = 'EABP_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EabpIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAG) and (column.Columnname = 'EAG_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EagIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAGT) and (column.Columnname = 'EAGT_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EagtIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEASE) and (column.Columnname = 'EASE_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EaseIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZPD) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPD_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZpdIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZPTDATEN);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENAVMP) and (column.Columnname = 'AVMP_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(AvmpIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAEAM) and (column.Columnname = 'EAEAM_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EaeamIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAEZ) and (column.Columnname = 'EAEZ_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EaezIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEPEXRI) and (column.Columnname = 'EPEXRI_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EpexriIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAZU) and (column.Columnname = 'EAZU_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EazuIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAZUD) and (column.Columnname = 'EAZUD_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EazudIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEAPO) and (column.Columnname = 'EAPO_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EapoIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENERG) and (column.Columnname = 'ERG_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ErgIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENEARG) and (column.Columnname = 'EARG_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(EargIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENZAEHLER);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENMLK) and (column.Columnname = 'MLK_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(MlkIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENMARKTLOKATION);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENZMLK) and (column.Columnname = 'ZMLK_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(ZMlkIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENMARKTLOKATION);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENTRE) and (column.Columnname = 'TRE_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(TreIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRES);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENTRD) and (column.Columnname = 'TRD_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(TrdIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRES);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENSRE) and (column.Columnname = 'SRE_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(SreIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRES);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENPRZ) and (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID')) then
  begin
    // es muss immer nach negierter PRZ_ID gesucht wird.
    if (PrzIdMapping.TryGetValue((StrToInt64(column.value) * -1), neueId)) then
    begin
      // falls vorhanden, mit negierter vorhanenden PRZ_ID mappen
      PrzIdMapping.Add(StrToInt64(column.value), (neueId * -1));
    end
    else
    begin
      // sonst neue anlegen
      FillIdMap(PrzIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENPROZESS);
    end;
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENRPR) and (column.Columnname = 'RPR_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(RprIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRECHNUNGSPROZESS);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENKNV) and (column.Columnname = 'KNV_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(KnvIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRECHNUNG);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENSPZ) and (column.Columnname = 'SPZ_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(SpzIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENSERVERPROZESS);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENVKZ) and (column.Columnname = 'VKZ_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(VkzIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRECHNUNG);
  end  
  else if ((tableName = ENVFA) and (column.Columnname = 'VFA_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(VfaIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRECHNUNG);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENVFAI) and (column.Columnname = 'VFAI_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(VfaiIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRECHNUNG);
  end
  else if ((tableName = ENVFME) and (column.Columnname = 'VFME_ID')) then
  begin
    FillIdMap(VfmeIdMapping, StrToInt64(column.value), SEQ_ENRECDRUCK);
  end

procedure TKundenImport.FillVbsNrZuIdMap;
var
  qry: TSQLQuery;
begin
  qry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Add('SELECT vbs_nr, vbs_id FROM ' + ENVBS);
    qry.Open;
    while (not qry.Eof) do
    begin
      fVbsNrZuId.Add(qry.FieldByName('vbs_nr').AsInteger, qry.FieldByName('vbs_id').AsInteger);
      qry.Next;
    end;
    qry.Close;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TKundenImport.FillIdMap(idMap: TIdMapping; orgId: Int64; seqName: TSequenceName): TSequenceInt;
begin
  if (not idMap.TryGetValue(orgId, Result)) then
  begin
    Result := ConnectionPool.GetSequenceValue(seqName);
    // Negative ProzessIDs müssen auch negativ bleiben
    if ((orgId < 0) and (orgId <> NULL_INTEGER)) then
    begin
      Result := Result * -1;
    end;
    idMap.Add(orgId, Result);
  end
  else
    Result := NULL_INTEGER;
end;

function TKundenImport.GetNextErvfNr: Integer;
var
  qry: TSQLQuery;
begin
  Result := NULL_INTEGER;
  qry := fConnection.CreateSQLQuery;
  try
    qry.SQL.Add('SELECT MAX(erv_nummer) FROM ' + ENERV + ' WHERE mnd_id = ' + IntToStr(FMndId));
    qry.Open;
    if (not qry.Eof) then
      Result := qry.Fields[0].AsInteger + 1;
    qry.Close;
  finally
    qry.Free;
  end;
end;

function TKundenImport.SchonImportiert(ATable: TTableObj): Boolean;
var
  i: Integer;
  id: Int64;
  column: TColumnObj;
  mvavKey, tableName : String;
  mapInfo: TTableIdMapping;
begin
  Result := False;
  tableName := ATable.Tablename;

  try
    if tableName = ENMVAV then
    begin
      mvavKey := GetMvavPk(ATable);
      result := mvavPkImportiert.indexOf(mvavKey) >= 0;
      exit;
    end;

    if TableIdMapping.ContainsKey(tableName) then
    begin
      mapInfo := TableIdMapping.Values[tableName];

      for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
      begin
        column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
        if (mapInfo.IdFieldName = column.Columnname) then
        begin
          Result := mapInfo.IdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.value), id);
          exit;
        end;
      end;
    end;


    for i := 0 to ATable.Columns.Count - 1 do
    begin
      column := ATable.Columns.Items[i];
      if ((tableName = ENABN) and (column.Columnname = 'ABN_ID')) then
        Result := AbnIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPT) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ID')) then
        Result := ZptIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENMES) and (column.Columnname = 'MES_ID')) then
        Result := MesIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENKND) and (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID')) then
        Result := KndIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEANL) and (column.Columnname = 'EANL_ID')) then
        Result := EanlIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEANB) and (column.Columnname = 'EANB_ID')) then
        Result := EanbIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAV) and (column.Columnname = 'EAV_ID')) then
        Result := EavIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAVS) and (column.Columnname = 'EAVS_ID')) then
        Result := EavsIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENMVA) and (column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID')) then
        Result := MvaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPO) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPO_ID')) then
        Result := ZpoIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPOE) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPOE_ID')) then
        Result := ZpoeIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPZ) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPZ_ID')) then
        Result := ZpzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPS) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPS_ID')) then
        Result := ZpsIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEZPA) and (column.Columnname = 'EZPA_ID')) then
        Result := EzpaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAD) and (column.Columnname = 'EAD_ID')) then
        Result := EadIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAPW) and (column.Columnname = 'EAPW_ID')) then
        Result := EapwIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)

      else if ((tableName = ENERG) and (column.Columnname = 'ERG_ID')) then

      else if ((tableName = ENEARG) and (column.Columnname = 'EARG_ID')) then

        Result := VfaiIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENVFME) and (column.Columnname = 'VFME_ID')) then
        Result := VfmeIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENGERP) and (column.Columnname = 'GERP_ID')) then
        Result := GerpIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENENVP) and (column.Columnname = 'ENVP_ID')) then
        Result := EnvpIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEANV) and (column.Columnname = 'EANV_ID')) then
        Result := EanvIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENMED) and (column.Columnname = 'MED_ID')) then
        Result := MedIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENABS) and (column.Columnname = 'ABS_ID')) then
        Result := AbsIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZMB) and (column.Columnname = 'ZMB_ID')) then
        Result := ZmbIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZLF) and (column.Columnname = 'ZLF_ID')) then
        Result := ZlfIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENPRZRA) and (column.Columnname = 'PRZRA_ID')) then
        Result := PrzraIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPG) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPG_ID')) then
        Result := ZpgIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEANLSP) and (column.Columnname = 'EANLSP_ID')) then
        Result := EanlspIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEASSB) and (column.Columnname = 'EASSB_ID')) then
        Result := eassbIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAAW) and (column.Columnname = 'EAAW_ID')) then
        Result := eaawIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAEB) and (column.Columnname = 'EAEB_ID')) then
        Result := eaebIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAES) and (column.Columnname = 'EAES_ID')) then
        Result := eaesIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAET) and (column.Columnname = 'EAET_ID')) then
        Result := eaetIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEATE) and (column.Columnname = 'EATE_ID')) then
        Result := eateIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEAVD) and (column.Columnname = 'EAVD_ID')) then
        Result := eavdIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if  ((tableName = ENTMV) and (column.Columnname = 'TMV_ID')) then
        Result := tmvIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPGD) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPGD_ID')) then
        Result := zpgdIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENGBZ) and (column.Columnname = 'GBZ_ID')) then
        Result := gbzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENERV) and (column.Columnname = 'ERV_ID')) then
        Result := ervIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENERVZP) and (column.Columnname = 'ERVZP_ID')) then
        Result := ervzpIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENERVA) and (column.Columnname = 'ERVA_ID')) then
        Result := ervaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENERVZT) and (column.Columnname = 'ERVZT_ID')) then
        Result := ervztIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPTTR) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPTTR_ID')) then
        Result := enzpttrIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPTTREANL) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPTTREANL_ID')) then
        Result := enzpttreanlIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENLSCH) and (column.Columnname = 'LSCH_ID')) then
        Result := enlschIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENLSMW) and (column.Columnname = 'LSMW_ID')) then
        Result := enlsmwIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENLSMH) and (column.Columnname = 'LSMH_ID')) then
        Result := enlsmhIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENVER) and (column.Columnname = 'VER_ID')) then
        Result := enverIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZLF) and (column.Columnname = 'ZLF_ID')) then
        Result := enzlfIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPK) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPK_ID')) then
        Result := enzpkIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENEANLBV) and (column.Columnname = 'EANLBV_ID')) then
        Result := eanlbvIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZTH) and (column.Columnname = 'ZTH_ID')) then
        Result := enzthIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENASTZ) and (column.Columnname = 'ASTZ_ID')) then
        Result := enastzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENPAV) and (column.Columnname = 'PAV_ID')) then
        Result := enpavIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAAUF) and (column.Columnname = 'AAUF_ID')) then
        Result := enaaufIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAAFE) and (column.Columnname = 'AAFE_ID')) then
        Result := enaafeIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAAUI) and (column.Columnname = 'AAUI_ID')) then
        Result := enaauiIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAAUS) and (column.Columnname = 'AAUS_ID')) then
        Result := enaausIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAAUB) and (column.Columnname = 'AAUB_ID')) then
        Result := enaaubIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAKA) and (column.Columnname = 'AKA_ID')) then
        Result := enakaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAPR) and (column.Columnname = 'APR_ID')) then
        Result := enaprIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENABM) and (column.Columnname = 'ABM_ID')) then
        Result := enabmIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENABK) and (column.Columnname = 'ABK_ID')) then
        Result := enabkIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENABZ) and (column.Columnname = 'ABZ_ID')) then
        Result := enabzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENABW) and (column.Columnname = 'ABW_ID')) then
        Result := enabwIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAAUW) and (column.Columnname = 'AAUW_ID')) then
        Result := enaauwIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAZZU) and (column.Columnname = 'AZZU_ID')) then
        Result := enazzuIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZSTE) and (column.Columnname = 'ZSTE_ID')) then
        Result := enzsteIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZSEF) and (column.Columnname = 'ZSEF_ID')) then
        Result := enzsefIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZSEI) and (column.Columnname = 'ZSEI_ID')) then
        Result := enzseiIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZMWE) and (column.Columnname = 'ZMWE_ID')) then
        Result := enzmweIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZMWZ) and (column.Columnname = 'ZMWZ_ID')) then
        Result := enzmwzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZSTEP) and (column.Columnname = 'ZSTEP_ID')) then
        Result := enzstepIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENAMMR) and (column.Columnname = 'AMMR_ID')) then
        Result := enammrIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENMWMEMA) and (column.Columnname = 'MWMEMA_ID')) then
        Result := enmwmemaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPTEM) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPTEM_ID')) then
        Result := enzptemIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id)
      else if ((tableName = ENZPTMA) and (column.Columnname = 'ZPTMA_ID')) then
        Result := enzptmaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(column.Value), id);
      if result then
        break;
    end;
  finally
    if (Result) then
      ATable.ImportErledigt := true;
  end;
end;

procedure TKundenImport.SetStatus(AStatusText: String);
begin
  if Assigned(FWartenThread) then
    FWartenThread.SetStatus(AStatusText);
end;

function TKundenImport.GetValue(column: TColumnObj; table: TTableObj; AObj: TExpImpTableObj; AForInsert: Boolean = true): String;
var
  orgValue, orgValueOhneHochkomma, tmpEanlNr: String;
  newId: Int64;
  i, j: Integer;
  kndEanlZuordnung: TKundenAnlagenZuordnung;
  jahrAbrBeginn, jahrRechnungsStichtagBis: Word;
  letzteTeilrechnungSoll: TDate;
  mapInfo, mapInfoVgl: TTableIdMapping;
  columnEa: TColumnObj;
  eneId: Integer;
  tableNameMapped,
  columnNameMapped: String;

begin
  Result := '';
  orgValue := column.Value;
  orgValueOhneHochkomma := EntferneHochkomma(orgValue);
  if (orgValue <> '') then
  begin
    if ((column.Columnname = 'ZPO_KONFIG_ID') or (column.Columnname = 'SMA_KONFIGID') or
        (column.Columnname = 'ZSTE_IMS_KONFIGID') or (column.Columnname = 'SMA_LOGICALNAME')) then
    begin
      if (not FZpoIdKonfig.TryGetValue(orgValueOhneHochkomma, Result)) then
      begin
        Result := QuotedStr('IMP' + IntToStr(ConnectionPool.GetSequenceValue(SEQ_ENZPTOBIS)));
        FZpoIdKonfig.AddObject(orgValueOhneHochkomma, Result);
      end;
      AddLog('Mapping: ' + column.Columnname + ' ' + orgValue + ': ersetzt durch ' + Result + '.');
      Exit;
    end
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZSTE_FKOBJEKT') then
    begin
      if (not FImportMaphelper.MeteringCodeMap.TryGetValue(orgValueOhneHochkomma, Result)) then
      begin
        Result := mapFachlichenSchluessel(table.Tablename, column.Columnname, orgValue);
        AddLog('Der ZSTE_FKOBJEKT "' + orgValueOhneHochkomma + '" wurde durch "' + EntferneHochkomma(Result) + '" ersetzt.');
        FImportMaphelper.RegisterMeteringCodeMapping(orgValueOhneHochkomma, EntferneHochkomma(Result));
      end
      else
      begin
        // Log Ausgabe nur für neu hinzugefügte Sätze. Der Satz hier ist bereits gemappt.
        result := QuotedStr(result);
      end;
      Exit;
    end;

    if (AObj <> nil) and AObj.GetNewColumnValue(table, column, self, result, AForInsert) then
    begin
      if result = '' then
        result := 'NULL';
      exit;
    end;

    columnNameMapped := column.Columnname;
    tableNameMapped := table.Tablename;

    if  (table.Tablename = ENPRZRA) then
    begin
      if column.Columnname = 'PRZRA_QUOTES_ID' then
      begin
        columnNameMapped := 'QUO_ID';
        tableNameMapped := EDQUO;
      end
       else
      if column.Columnname = 'PRZRA_ORD_ID' then
      begin
        columnNameMapped := 'ORD_ID';
        tableNameMapped := EDORD;
      end
    end;

    newId := NULL_INTEGER;
    mapInfo := nil;
    for mapInfoVgl in TableIdMapping.ValueCollection do
      if mapInfoVgl.IsRelevant(tableNameMapped, columnNameMapped) then
      begin
        mapInfo := mapInfoVgl;
        break;
      end;

    if (mapInfo <> nil) then
    begin
      Result := 'NULL';
      if (mapInfo.IdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValueOhneHochkomma), newId)) then
      begin
        if (newId <> NULL_INTEGER) and (newId <> 0) then
            Result := IntToStr(newId);
        exit;
      end;
    end;

    if (table.Tablename = ENNWS) and (column.Columnname = 'NBT_ID_NEU') then
    begin
      columnNameMapped := 'NBT_ID';
      tableNameMapped := ENNWS;
    end;

    if (table.Tablename = ENNWMN) and (column.Columnname = 'NWMN_EMPFAENGER_ID') then
    begin
      if (table.GetColumnByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_LIEFERANT)) then
      begin
        columnNameMapped := 'LIF_ID';
        tableNameMapped := ENNWMN;
      end;
      if (table.GetColumnByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_NETZBETREIBER)) then
      begin
        columnNameMapped := 'NBT_ID';
        tableNameMapped := ENNWMN;
      end;
      if (table.GetColumnByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_MESSSTELLENBETREIBER)) then
      begin
        columnNameMapped := 'MBT_ID';
        tableNameMapped := ENNWMN;
      end;
      if (table.GetColumnByName('NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP').Value = IntToStr(NWMN_EMPFAENGER_TYP_UEBERTRAGUNGSNETZBETREIBER)) then
      begin
        columnNameMapped := 'MPT_ID';
        tableNameMapped := ENNWMN;
      end;
    end;

    // Auflösung über Stammdaten
    if FSdManager.CanResolve(columnNameMapped) then
    begin
      if FImportOptionen.StammdatenWieExport then
        result := orgValue // Keine Änderung der Stammdaten
      else
      begin
        result := FSdManager.GetValue(columnNameMapped, orgValue);
        if result = '' then
        begin
          result := FSdManager.GetDefaultValue(columnNameMapped, orgValue, fMndId);
          if (result <> '') then
          begin
            AddLog(column.Columnname + ' ' + orgValue + ': Standardwert ' + result + ' verwendet');
            FSdManager.AddDefaultValue(columnNameMapped, orgValue, Result);
          end;
        end;
        if (result = '') then
          if (not FImportOptionen.FehlerIgnorieren) then
          begin
            raise Exception.Create('Für die Stammdaten zur Spalte ' + table.Tablename + '.' + column.Columnname + ' konnte für den Alt-Wert ' + orgValue + ' weder eine Übersetzung noch ein Vorgabewert gefunden werden!');
          end;
      end;

      // wenn über Stammdaten aufgelöst, dann mit dem Result aussteigen
      if (result <> '') then
        exit;
    end;

    if (column.Columnname = 'SMA_KONFIGID') or (column.Columnname = 'ZPO_KONFIG_ID') then
    begin
      result := GetMappedKonfigId(orgValue);
      exit;
    end;

    if (column.Columnname = 'NBT_NR') and (table.Tablename = ENKNV) then
    begin
      result := '';
      if (orgValue <> '') then
      begin
        for j := 0 to table.Columns.Count - 1 do
        begin
          columnEa := table.Columns.Items[j];
          if (columnEa.Columnname = 'KNV_ENERGIEART') then
          begin
            eneId := StrToIntDef(columnEa.Value, NULL_INTEGER);
            if eneId <> NULL_INTEGER then
              result := FSdManager.ResolveNbtNr(column.Value, eneId);
            break;
          end;
        end;
        if result = '' then
          AddLog(column.Columnname + ' ' + orgValue + ': Nicht auflösbar, NULL verwendet');
      end;
    end
     else
    if (column.Columnname = 'ZZW_ID') then
    begin
      AddLog(column.Columnname + ' ' + orgValue + ': NULL verwendet');
     // ZZW_ID kommt aus V3, z.T. gibts hier aber evtl. noch DB-Constraints
      exit;
    end
     else
    if (column.Columnname = 'ZZW_ID') then
    begin
      AddLog(column.Columnname + ' ' + orgValue + ': NULL verwendet');
     // ZZW_ID kommt aus V3, z.T. gibts hier aber evtl. noch DB-Constraints
      exit;
    end
     else
    if (column.Columnname = 'AGSP_ID') and (result = '') then
    begin
       AddLog(column.Columnname + ' ' + orgValue + ': NULL verwendet');
      // ENAGSP wird aktuell noch nicht exportiert/fachlich importiert
      exit;
    end
     else
    if ((column.Columnname = 'ZPO_KONFIG_ID') and (table.Tablename = ENZPO)) then
    begin
      // zpo_konfig_id in ENSMA auflösen
      mapInfo := TableIdMapping.Values[ENSMA];
      if (mapInfo <> nil) then
      begin
        Result := NULL_STRING;
        try
          if (mapInfo.IdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(EntferneHochkomma(orgValue)), newId)) then
          begin
            if ((newId <> NULL_INTEGER) and (newId <> 0)) then
              Result := IntToStr(newId);
          end;
        except
          On E : Exception Do begin
            // nichts machen
            // wenn das Feld in Marktdatenaustausch gefüllt ist, steht hier ein String
            // und es gibt keine Referenz auf SMA_ID. In diesem Fall 1:1 String importieren
          end;
        end;
        if (Result = NULL_STRING) then
          Result := column.Value;
      end;
      exit;
    end
    else
    // Sync-Nummern dürfen nie importiert werden
    if (column.Columnname = 'ABN_SYNCNR') or
       (column.Columnname = 'ZPK_SYNCNR') or
       (column.Columnname = 'GERP_SYNCNR') then
     result := ''
    else
    if (column.Columnname = 'ABN_ID') then
      AbnIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ID') then
      ZptIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPTH_ABRECHNUNGSZPT_ID') then
      ZptIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ID_MALO') then
      ZptIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPT_ID_TRANCHE') then
      ZptIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'MES_ID') then
      MesIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'KND_ID') then
      KndIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EANL_ID') then
      EanlIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EANB_ID') then
      EanbIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAV_ID') then
      EavIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAVS_ID') then
      EavsIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'MVA_ID') then
      MvaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPO_ID') then
      ZpoIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPOE_ID') then
      ZpoeIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPZ_ID') then
      ZpzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPS_ID') then
      ZpsIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EZPA_ID') then
      EzpaIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAD_ID') then
      EadIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAPW_ID') then
      EapwIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPB_ID') then
      ZpbIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPBD_ID') then
      ZpbdIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPN_ID') then
      ZpnIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAB_ID') then
      EabIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EABT_ID') then
      EabtIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EABP_ID') then
      EabpIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAG_ID') then
      EagIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAGT_ID') then
      EagtIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EASE_ID') then
      EaseIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZPD_ID') then
      ZpdIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'AVMP_ID') then
      AvmpIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAEAM_ID') then
      EaeamIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAEZ_ID') then
      EaezIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EPEXRI_ID') then
      EpexriIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAZU_ID') then
      EazuIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAZUD_ID') then
      EazudIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EAPO_ID') then
      EapoIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ERG_ID') then
      ErgIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'EARG_ID') then
      EargIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'MLK_ID') then
      MlkIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'ZMLK_ID') then
      ZMlkIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if ((column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') or (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_IDBASIS') or (column.Columnname = 'ABSZ_REPRZ_ID')) then
    begin
      PrzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId);
      AddLog('PRZ_ID - alt = ' + orgValue + ' ===> PRZ_ID - neu = ' + IntToStr(newId));
    end
    else if (column.Columnname = 'RPR_ID') then
      RprIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
    else if (column.Columnname = 'KNV_ID') then
      KnvIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)

          if (FImportOptionen.LetztesRechnungsdatumAufNull) then
            Result := 'NULL'
          else if (FImportOptionen.RechnungenImportieren) then
          begin
            if ((FImportOptionen.StichtagBis <> NULL_DATE) and (getDateFromValue(column.Value) >= FImportOptionen.StichtagBis)) then
            begin
              Result := dtmLib32SystemZeit.getDatabaseDateTimeString(FImportOptionen.StichtagBis);
              if (FImportOptionen.LetzteRechnungNurTeilRechnung) then
              begin
                if (column.Columnname = 'MES_LETZTERECHNUNG') then
                begin
                  jahrRechnungsStichtagBis := YearOf(FImportOptionen.StichtagBis);
                  jahrAbrBeginn := YearOf(fAbrBeginn);
                  if (jahrRechnungsStichtagBis > jahrAbrBeginn) then
                    Result := dtmLib32SystemZeit.getDatabaseDateTimeString(EncodeDate(jahrRechnungsStichtagBis - 1, 12, 31))
                  else
                    Result := 'NULL';
                end
                else if ((column.Columnname = 'MES_LETZTETEILRECHNUNG') and (fAnlagenAbrechnungsart = 2)) then
                begin
                  letzteTeilrechnungSoll := FImportOptionen.StichtagBis;
                  letzteTeilrechnungSoll := IncMonth(letzteTeilrechnungSoll, -1);
                  Result := dtmLib32SystemZeit.getDatabaseDateTimeString(letzteTeilrechnungSoll);
                end
              end;
            end
            else
              Result := orgValue;
          end
          else
          begin
            Result := orgValue;
          end;
        end
        else
        begin
          Result := orgValue;
        end;
      end
      else if (table.Tablename = ENEPEXRI) then
      begin
        if (column.Columnname = 'PRZ_ID') then
        begin
          if (FImportOptionen.RechnungenImportieren) then
            PrzIdMapping.TryGetValue(StrToInt64(orgValue), newId)
          else
            Result := '0'
        end
        else
          Result := orgValue;
      end
       else
      if result = '' then
        result := orgValue;
    end;

    if (newId <> NULL_INTEGER) then
    begin
      if (newId = 0) then
